- `hmh07-lua:195` (original lines 32-32, 32-38): Common Hud constructs Stuck directly. No writes to a damage/stuck gameplay flag are present after initialization, no close override in span. Optional postload then self return. Original enum/image producer and asset lifetime remain unknown.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:046` (original lines 787-831, 32-38, 32-38): Initializes six controller progress/status models only when absent, then creates dynent/heal/revive/being-revived/bleedout progress and text/prompt children. Healing requires powerRatio>0, ability in-use and player not dead; Reviving uses reviveProgress>0, BeingRevived equality1, CowardsWayOut exact setting plus last-stand/not-ended/not-finished guards, Deposit both dynent progress and depositing>0. Ratio updates write the exact shader data; reviveProgress callback delegates smooth interpolation only for nonnil values. All status changes request state reevaluation. Close registration/postload precede SetupReviveProgressBar and SetupBleedoutProgressBar, then self return. This consumes combat state and does not implement revive/deposit timing or native cancellation.

- `hmh07-lua:023` (original lines 364-370): Binds item image/description/name from DirectorZMLoadoutPreview. Restriction icon/text can become visible only outside director pregame and public WZ lobby, and when the restricted model flag is true. Thus this restriction-display path is source-excluded under IsPublicWZLobby, despite common construction. Whole widget Invisible follows info-shown false; TraditionalChinesePC has a distinct predicate/clip. No ZM loadout gameplay semantics are inferred.

  status: complete_bounded_contract

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:235` (original lines 32-38, 623-628, 1011-1013, 611-621): Creates a controller-owned full-screen KillcamMenu_WZ, initializes killcamAllowRespawn only if unset, requests menu-open sound and constructs the displayed deathcam children/assets. Registers Killcam state from the visibility bit. Its close wrapper stops the one-shot sound then requests team_eliminated music; scriptNotify callback plays EndTransition only for post_killcam_transition. Processes menu_loaded, registers class close hook, optionally calls PostLoadFunc, writes ignoreCursor and starts the one-shot sound before returning self. All hooks/subscriptions remain deferred; no respawn/network transaction is proved.

- `hmh07-lua:186` (original lines 360-360, 360-362, 654-654, 654-663, 1011-1011, 1011-1013): Hud_WZ directly constructs this result widget. SetupGameEndScoreWidget and DisableAutoButtonCallback run first; demo.processedKeyframeJump and lobbyRoot.publicLobby.stage are initialized to0 (real model writes, not backend stage proof). Builds result/outcome/eliminator/status/timer/deathcam/VOIP/quit/placement/killer/weapon/AAR/director/crosshair children. The constructor requests a GameScore/titleString subscription. Only if its callback is delivered and model:get() is nonnil, it requests LocalizeToUpperString with that value and passes the unknown result to Outcome:setText, if earlier accesses/calls succeed; nil takes no explicit setText arm. Callback delivery, localization/case result and displayed effect remain unresolved; aliveTeammateCount drives DownNotOut art; GameScore model forwarded to eliminator/challenge/killer/weapon displays. Victory controls challenge coin visibility. meansOfDeath>0 hides killer/weapon displays and inversely reveals self-caused/means-of-death displays. Attacker identity model rebinding first removes and nils old per-field subscription, dereferences new attackerClientModel, then subscribes xuid/clientNum/rank/rankIcon; analogous clientModel rebinding populates MistakesWereMade identity. Calling card receives full client model; nonnil callbacks update emblem/name/cleaned clan/rank/icon. No nil update clears old displayed text explicitly. VOIP HudStart has a recovered early-return/branch shape (exact trace) and ShowForCodCaster AlwaysFalse; do not replace it with an assumed visibility formula. Outcome state family: ShowOutcomeTransition with no killcam -> Victory/PC; defeat/team_elimination with no killcam -> Defeat/GameOver variants; elimination -> Elimination. Platoon variants additionally platoonCount!=0; PC variants additionally IsPC. Non-PC named states do not themselves contain not-IsPC, so do not assume exclusivity; LUI arbitration stays external. State/current/visibility/profile/lobby/teammate/AAR/input subscriptions reevaluate state/buttons. Square prioritizes console spectate (hide AAR/send skip_deathcam/clear transition/location/hide occluder), then console online AAR close under ui_showWZAAR/noncustom/not-spectate. Triangle redeploy requires private host, all members can redeploy, no matchmaking, redeploy_enabled and noncustom. Cross console deathcam requires eligibility, non-PC, not alternate killcam button, not UI-active/demo/default and viewed report; sends play_deathcam, sets hasPlayedDeathCam, plays alias/music. Circle console hold-finished quits; PC branches quit on non-held conditions; contextual2 handles PC exit. Each action returns true when taken, label callbacks often return false despite valid action, exact source retained. setState CallOriginalFirst hook branches demo/console/PC-occluded focus/intro/cursor/chat behavior; keyframeJump clears transition only during demo. Close is registered via CallOriginalSecond to an unselected __onClose; optional postload then focus/safe-area setup and return. Assets, backend result/rank/AAR provenance, hook order/cleanup outside scope, native menu response and actual execution remain open.

RESET_LOADOUTS only for a nonhost first force-notifies closePopups, then PC calls ResetEverythingOnPC; other platforms initialize PlayerSettingsUpdate, open/reset/close the controls overlay on controller 0 and request default offline MP and second hashed storage loadouts using the primary controller. Reads MP online stats and the entitlement-indexed hashed field; returns field==1 when present, nil when buffer/entry is missing. When the MP online stats buffer and entitlement field exist, sets the field to1, requests storagewrite, and returns true. Missing input returns nil. True records the request path, not durable save or server confirmation. Creates the controller EntitlementData model, indexes EntitlementDisplayInfo by the supplied entitlement, and writes entitlement, displayName, description, and addsCODPoints in that order. Missing display metadata is not guarded. First populates popup data, then opens EntitlementPopupEreg for the exact hash_12EDA6DC2D0D4A00 entitlement or EntitlementPopup for all others. The popup target/provider and successful display remain separate. Requests AddToTableIfNotEmpty for every enumerated DOTDImages value. Available baseutility859-863 rejects only the literal hash_0 sentinel; it does not filter empty strings or false keys, and ordinary Lua nil keys can error. It then replaces ForceStreamedEntitlementImages with the ForceStreamHelper result, retaining the old stream helper value as input. Returns true for development build plus the supplied name_force dvar string1, otherwise for positive DOTD incentive-token count plus opaque status query500200==0, otherwise false. No branch is removed merely because it is development-related.

  status: complete_bounded_contract

  Complete explanatory fields: [exact projection](../../provenance/documentation/34b42b2a2e564be5-001.json). Older compact summaries and original evidence remain unchanged.

  Exact excerpt: [saved claim](../../provenance/modules/34b42b2a2e564be5.json) at JSON pointer `/claims/72`.

  Archived evidence ID: `archived-record-00219` at JSON pointer ``; file SHA-256 `75ca3ecdfb02edadb417c3124987ec397aa8375eda735805c5b7f7b71416b839`.

- `hmh07-lua:211` (original lines 10-10, 10-12, 19-19, 19-21): ui/main.lua1127..1144 directly builds common Hud when no existing T7HudMenu and gametype is neither doa nor codfu; it is separate from later WZ-specific Hud_WZ factory selection, so common Hud dependencies are not excluded by an MP-looking child. Constructor resets displayTop3Players/isInPrematchPeriod0, creates showSpawnSelect, disables keyboard navigation and initializes in-game contract record. Builds armor/back/front safe area, waypoint/overhead containers, out-of-bounds/prematch/stuck/proximity/crate/thrust/sprint/spectate/console/faction/subtitle children. Back/front menu_loaded handlers run local/super menuLoaded, SizeToSafeArea, then dispatch to children only if unhandled. OutOfBounds follows IsOutOfBounds and endtime/display updates. It replaces DeadSpectate Visible/VisiblePC with visibility-bit predicates additionally not IsWarzone (PC variant also IsPC); therefore those EXACT caller states are excluded when IsWarzone=true, though the constructor still runs. Self HideWaypoints if game-ended or missing showobjicons or opaque bit/flash; HideWaypointsAndNames when showSpawnSelect equals1; Multiplayer delegates IsMultiplayer. Relevant model updates reevaluate. The registered hud_boot callback initializes a nil local, requests ShouldBootUpHUD and retains both empty recovered then/else arms425-429. If the preceding accesses/calls succeed, its still-falsey local takes the dispatchEventToChildren request and the callback returns that result. No direct alpha assignment is present in those arms; provider effects and original recovery/VM fidelity remain unknown; scoreboard Back prioritizes CanShowScoreboard; hide requires CanHideScoreboard AND not Warzone. The registered scriptNotify callback tests player_spawned with prematch/not-Campaign predicates at458/460, but both matched recovered arms are empty and supply no direct alpha write; create_prematch_timer is not-Warzone, prematch_timer_ended clears flag, draft_complete creates timer+TryBootHUD, two opaque contract hashes call record/update helpers. These notifications are registrations, not proof of server production or progression persistence. menu_loaded processed, CallOriginalSecond close hook registered, then PreLoadFunc (near end), waypoint/overhead handlers initialized, m_inputDisabled and preserved Back set, cursor disabled, waypoint parent setup, optional PC spectate sizing, return self. Unselected class clips/close and external helpers remain source-ready dependencies; model/native/VM/build/runtime limits remain.

It does not clamp the counter or close the menu. Requests menu_go_back sound only if a sound set exists, then returns returnToPreviousMenu(controller). The native/menu-method implementation is external. Nil/empty input or missing dvar returns no values. Otherwise tokenizes non-space/comma runs, excludes every matching name, rejoins remaining tokens with comma-space, then writes the dvar. Returns no values if navigation tables are absent; otherwise searches nested histories and earlier entries for the two names, returning the two found indices. All three recovered numeric headers explicitly end at1 with step-1; their respective starts are #f96_local0, #f96_local4 and f96_local5-1. Original VM/table behavior remains unproved. Passes record_curr_focused_elem_id with controller and disableUpdateCursorFocus to the receiver method, then returns true; no explicit target element ID is provided. Passes record_inputfocus_elem_id and controller to RecordCurrFocusedElemID, then falls through without an explicit return.

- `hmh07-lua:239` (original lines not specified): The supplied factory self-definition is registration, not an observed invocation. Conditional factory contract: create DirectorFindGameWZ menu, set user-data properties, SetupDirectorFiltersCards, owner/sound, build selected info and 3x4 OptionsList using DirectorPlaylistOption. Confirm consumes over-max-party input without action; otherwise unlocked item runs ProcessListAction then GoBack and returns true; locked falls through. Label over-max/locked returns false, unlocked selects true. Selected info links active option model; tab grid dataSource changes replace OptionsList datasource. Footer/header/backing/tabbar menu_loaded handlers call local/super handler, safe-area logic (footer only non-PC) then dispatch unhandled events to children. Tabs Hidden=not MultiplePlaylistTabsAvailable, reevaluated on grid/input changes; tabs use DirectorFilters with8 columns. list_active_changed gives focus to menu default. Back button calls GoBack and returns true. ClearMenuSavedState is hooked through the named close override via CallOriginalFirst; self.__onClose is hooked before original via CallOriginalSecond, with exact implementation retained in qualified companion. PC setup/restore is conditional. menu_loaded is processed before final optional postload; SetModelFromPropertyModel and ignoreSavedActive=true occur last, then self returned. No live matchmaking request is proved; native factory dispatch/models/state and exact HMH tab/filter membership stay open.

Empty state or an existing timer falls through without either action; actual timer delivery/provider behavior remains unverified; other branches delay in-game report. GetEliminatedString chooses exact hash by arg1==0. HasViewed returns true offline else stored aarViewed. Native/UI delivery and effective settings remain unproved.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:104` (original lines 885-892, 535-544, 535-544): CanShowAAR requires both stats records and one allowed menu state, resolves current gametype/fallback hash, checks a gametype stats entry, then mode-specific validity: zombies excludes tutorial; WZ compares TIME_PLAYED_TOTAL old<new; other modes require AAR.valid==1. ForceAAR is only the elseif fallback, requiring current stats and allowed menu state. OpenAARIfNeeded creates viewedAAR, returns when already true, otherwise postpones/forces images and schedules next-frame work; streamed images open immediately, otherwise50ms timer accumulates timeElapsed until >6000 or streamed, resumes streams, closes spinner, opens report, clears saved state and closes timer. viewedAAR is set true after scheduling. Ineligible public/arena invokes match survey. OpenAAR prioritizes WZ, zombies, arena, then MP overlay. Missing models, callback cancellation and backend validity are not supplied.

  unknowns: &#91;"Per-body nil/error/return/lifetime limits are explicit in ordered operations; no universal cleanup, cancellation or exception recovery is inferred.", "Recovered-source fidelity defects remain visible; saved AST/receipt checks are not independent correctness review."&#93;

  status: complete_bounded_contract

  Complete explanatory fields: [exact projection](../../provenance/documentation/34b42b2a2e564be5-001.json). Older compact summaries and original evidence remain unchanged.

  Exact excerpt: [saved claim](../../provenance/modules/34b42b2a2e564be5.json) at JSON pointer `/claims/113`.

  Archived evidence ID: `archived-record-00036` at JSON pointer `/units/103`; file SHA-256 `279ba12c6a1d4cfd1fb6c7e030a47694d4ee3982256bb21a0b3aa6909aef143b`; selected record SHA-256 `ae137128b156f2f0665d617515467cae912d7fb992d5064b37d53ba2a6ef2979`.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:273` (original lines 448-454, 434-446, 448-454): With a cursor, computes bounds centered on the target. Animated path plays Release, begins50ms movement, updates bounds, and registers its focus/selection helper on transition completion; direct path updates bounds and calls it now. That helper chooses Snap/Acquire, sends gain_focus, stores selectedRegion and sends SpawnRegionFocus only for the written lobby mode or simulatedCT. Requests target lose_focus then DefaultClip, clears selectedRegion and hides cursor only when the keep-visible argument is falsy. Gets the stick vector, negates y in that same table and stores it on the receiver. Collects beacons whose native hit-test contains the cursor, computes squared center distance, sorts by ascending distance with its owned comparator, and returns first marker or nil. Equal-distance tie order is unspecified. Missing SpawnSelectionMap returns. Found target optionally deselects old region, selects new region and plays uin_main_edit. No target leaves selection unchanged. Returns whether native objective name equals spawnbeacon_objective. Non-sentinel objective ID compares native gamemode flags to f0_local1, otherwise false. Sentinel/flag upvalue values are outside this selected body and remain context gaps. Non-sentinel objective ID compares native gamemode flags to f0_local2, otherwise false. Exact upvalue values remain outside this body scope. For each beacon subscription, reads active/team/beacon/group predicates. Eligible missing beacon requests add; existing eligible beacon requests remove then add and a recovered goto. A remove follows the outer eligible block literally; do not invent a missing label/continue. PC keyboard-prompt branch preserves empty arms and local-looking assignments. Control-flow fidelity remains unresolved. Returns receiver.killcamFullscreen unchanged, with no boolean normalization. Returns whether killcamAnimTimer differs from nil. Truthy timer is closed before its receiver field becomes nil. No implicit guarantee of close success or idempotence beyond the written guard. Replaces beacons/subscription tables; existing AutoSpawnMarker is closed/selection cleared when hideautospawnoption==1, else made focusable and stored under sentinel; objId is written either way. AnimateMarkerPulse is called even with nil local marker. Installs newObjective subscription only if absent. Its delivered callback ignores active killcam and handles owned spawn-beacon/group models; death-marker/other arms are empty in recovered text. No old beacon table disposal occurs before replacement. Iterates beaconSubs and requests RemoveSpawnBeacon for each key. It does not directly close/remove newObjSubscription in this body. Starts with AutoSpawnMarker and replaces candidate for every non--1 pairs entry, so last traversal entry wins. Changed candidate deselects old, then selects/snaps a present candidate. An unchanged auto marker is selected again. No deterministic ordering is invented. Reads parent size and child rectangle, writes normalized X/Y and width(+20)/parent-width scale to shared KillcamPip fields. No division-by-zero guard or height scale. Installs spawnSelectEndTime model callback. A nonnil time records end/wait values and creates one50ms timer if absent. Its tick updates Clock/ClockGlow shader fractions from remaining/wait time and closes/nils the timer at remaining<=0. No initial immediate tick, nil-time cancellation, wait-zero guard or widget-close hook is supplied. Defaults false, sets true for sentinel objId, and also true for a present model whose state is active. It does not clear sentinel true for an inactive model.

- `hmh07-lua:067` (original lines 10-12): Sets CodCaster/prompt models and delayed menus, creates/tracks toast, temporarily resets playerSpawned, reuses or creates shared HUD, and selects Hud_WZ when Warzone or gametype warzone. CodCaster MP branch explicitly excludes Warzone. Restores spawn model, attaches/opens/focuses mode HUD, updates score/demo widgets and removes old draft subscription. PC+Warzone gets occlusion handlers; draft-stage subscription is multiplayer-gated. Keep engine flags/alias consistency and private helper bodies unresolved.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:033` (original lines 386-388, 386-388): Creates Hud_MP_Codcaster menu, safe-area child and MP HUD components, current-weapon damage model binding, score notifications and spectator/profile state consumers. On score_event it prioritizes bounty score, then combat-efficiency score when spawn selection is inactive, then normal score. Objective ShowNotification predicates are AlwaysFalse here. HideAllButScoreboard is killcam/final/round/stage visibility union; SpeedBoost uses exact gadget id/state; HideForCodCaster requires spectator profile. X input is blocked only while AreXButtonPressesDisallowed; player_spawned outside draft disallows X400ms. Menu_loaded sizes PC to HUD area and console to safe area, dispatches to children when unhandled; then focus/state restoration, close registration, postload, back-button preservation, grenade warnings and cursor disable occur in literal order. Menu selection into this MP codcaster path remains conditional; no universal HMH requirement is inferred.

- `hmh07-lua:231` (original lines 932-932, 932-936, 32-32, 32-38, 654-654, 654-663, 665-665, 665-670, 1015-1015, 1015-1023): Supplied caller anchor is the Director factory definition itself, so external invocation/HMH menu navigation is unproven. Conditional constructor creates menu, setups default-focus callbacks/any-controller/offline-screen/build-info/companion-app/contracts model, then builds theater/custom/training/arena/public/private/pregame/select and shared/PC-looking children. Direct construction is not authority to reconstruct every other-mode child. Child menu_loaded wrappers use local/super menuLoaded, conditional non-PC safe-area sizing, then dispatch only if unhandled. State predicates: HideForGameStart from LobbyRoot hideMenusForGameStart1; ShowSelectKorea15Plus requires PC+Zombies+opaque Korea eligibility; other states delegate exact DirectorUtility ShowDirectorSelect/Pregame/Private/Custom/Arena/ArenaMatchmaking/Theater/Public/Training. lobbyNav/showSelect/hide flags reevaluate state; network/client/input/xuid/time/spinner/stage/session changes refresh button state. unused_gamepad_button invokes LobbyAddLocalClient(event.controller or owner) then dispatches children. Non-PC Start opens StartMenu_Main; non-PC social requires online/nonLAN/nonguest/allowed/not-demo-disabled/not-training, while PC select Triangle activates featured. Square branches offline-demo LAN hold behavior or nonPC select featured activation. Escape/mouse-keyboard and Circle/gamepad have separate ordered chains: hide preview, clear selected client, cancel/leave applicable custom/arena/theater/public states subject to spinner/timer/host/eligibility, or PC select quit. Duplicate preview branches are preserved and may be shadowed by earlier identical predicates; no guessed simplification. Back opens AAR only when CanShowAAR and refreshes prompt. RTrigger gestures requires dvar lobby_gestures_enabled plus opaque hash_61E20A632CB63092; then alias/preserve-button/open selector. setState CallOriginalFirst hook conditionally opens AAR/refreshes contract/client updates or initializes training; external predicates keep non-HMH internals conditional. Close CallOriginalFirst hook clears saved state and OnDirectorClose; class __onClose registered via CallOriginalSecond outside selected body remains dependency. lobby room/isMatchmaking callbacks refresh room; hideMenusForGameStart1 plays hide clip+loses focus/hides cursor,0 plays default/unhides cursor. menu_loaded then optional restore/postload; PC branch additionally key-color/friends/Korea/navigation/ZM-availability hooks, console branch DirectorUtility.PostLoad only. Exact default-focus registrations occur at end, then self returned. No live lobby/account action was executed; backend/model/enum/native dispatch, actual visibility and clip/close bodies remain open.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:005` (original lines 40-46): The constructor requests persistent seenWZInGameAAR initialization only when its child handle is falsey, and prepares merit reward models with false, then requests WZAAR_Containers and an empty focus target. Visible requires the aarVisible model value to be literal true; the registered model subscription requests reevaluation only when delivered. The registered setState callback, when delivered and its Visible predicate succeeds, requests LockInputHUD(self,true), MenuHidesFreeCursor(menu,delivered controller), then SetupMeritModels(true). MenuHidesFreeCursor does work only when usingfreecursor is truthy: it handles a prior delayed timer, may register close/unhide, and if hidFreeCursor is falsey sets that flag and either clears truthy ignoreNextMenuHides or requests hidden:get()+1. Actual cursor/native result is unknown. The DefaultState branch requests LockInputHUD(false) and lose focus. It registers close, may request PostLoadFunc, and returns only if reached. No independent close/unlock-on-error guarantee, callback delivery/order, persistent seen/AAR/backend provenance or runtime behavior is established.

- `hmh07-lua:184` (original lines 32-32, 32-38, 1011-1011, 1011-1013): ui/main.lua1132..1165 builds common Hud unless existing/excluded gametype, then chooses WZ suffix for CoD.isWarzone OR gametype warzone (Campaign and CODFU/DOA overrides retained), and only when no T7HudMenuGameMode invokes Hud_WZ through dynamic factory unless non-WZ codcaster special case applies. Original effective flags/build remain conditional. Constructor initializes aarVisible/aarViewed only if unset, disabling automatic buttons only on PC, creates safe-area/ready/damage/chat/objective/results/scoreboard/hint/wheel/PC widgets. ReadyEvents scriptNotify dispatches hero_weapon_received/killstreak_received/levelup helpers; objective ShowNotification defaults are AlwaysFalse. MPHintText nonempty predicate and nonnil text link make this shared MP-named child usable on WZ. Sprays Visible requires held button plus no prematch-hide/last-stand/inventory/multi-pickup/remote/vehicle/demo/dead/round-end/scoreboard/spectator exclusions; Callouts similarly requires pressed+enabled and no theater/prematch/inventory/last-stand/dead/round-end/scoreboard/spectator exclusion. All corresponding model/input links reevaluate state, never produce gameplay input by themselves. Self states: HideAllButScoreboard=stage_ended; SpeedBoost=gadget_speed_burst plus gadget2 inuse; HideForCodCaster=caster with profile playerhud1. Circle/back hides scoreboard only through predicates and blocks the game event. Up opens quick inventory only with HUD weapon visibility, allowed inventory, no ended/demo/dead/scoreboard/spectating/UI/laststand/vehicle-hide/disable-setting; Down closes with its separate narrower gate. Weapon-next/prev/primary/secondary require Warzone, full inventory open and no demo. LB/RB/triggers and Cross prioritize health/equipment cycle actions when quick inventory open/profile cycle-items1/button-mapping agrees; they block the game event. Cross then has PC-gamepad deathcam fallback. PC-only spectate/deathcam/AAR/report branches retain exact additional conditions; if IsPC=false they do not execute, without excluding unconditional PC-looking child construction. CanUseQuickInventory notification closes quick inventory only if now disallowed and already open; character_unlock_update dispatches UpdateUnlockQuestState. menu_loaded processed, linked __onClose hook registered, captured f0_local0 postload invoked if present. PC final setup adds region checks/sizing; console final setup disables keyboard navigation and adds WZ armblade reticles; both setup grenade warnings/custom focus/cursor rules. Return self. Full exact callbacks/labels/branch ordering are in trace; unselected class clips/reset/close and external helper bodies are dependencies only, with no claim of original event delivery, network menu response or real execution.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:123` (original lines 535-544, 535-544, 535-544): Stores a ListSetup datasource under the supplied name and returns that name. The owned provider, only when invoked, builds image model rows from each supplied item whose chosen field exists, using current session mode and item-image requests. Passing the provider is distinct from executing it. Returns 0 offline. Online, scans all balance items for matching name, keeps the last match, subtracts a positive supplied adjustment and clamps below zero. It reads the AARPreMatch baseline when that field exists and returns the nonnegative difference. Recovered f59_local1..4 assignments lack local declarations here; no lexical repair is made. Returns without changes when Black Market is disabled. Otherwise loops the selected stream tier items, separates signature-weapon/outfit/gesture properties, rewrites weaponRef/rarity/unlocked/isLootCase, clears moved fields and appends models/properties rows unless the optional name filter rejects them. The local bribe-name predicate is defined then called immediately for each item; it is not a deferred service callback. The optional rarity dvar overrides the computed rarity. The fake-tier path returns currentTier minus three and 200 when the needed model exists. Otherwise uses AARPreMatch tiers/credits, replacing tiers with currentSeasonTiers when present; absent baseline data delegates to GetLootStreamData. Only two values are returned. Fake-tier mode returns currentTier, 800, lootTierMax and 1 when the current-tier model exists. Normal data returns currentTier/currentLootXP/lootTierMax and redeemed skips or 0. A missing progress/currentTier path returns four zeroes. Other child lookups in a present path are not individually guarded. Returns false outside public online play or with Black Market disabled. Otherwise the forced AAR overlay or LootCaseRewardUp returns true; the remaining path compares prematch tier against current tier. It does not grant rewards.
